## 🌟 Pourquoi postuler ici ?
Trouver le bon poste est difficile. Nous faisons le tri en récupérant les meilleures opportunités.

- **💰 Rémunération Top 1%:** Postes à partir de 40$/h et dépassant les 100$/h.
- **🌍 100% à Distance:** Travaillez d'où vous voulez avec vos propres horaires.

> 🤝 **Note de Transparence:** Les liens fournis sont des liens de parrainage. Si vous êtes embauché, nous pouvons gagner une petite commission sans aucun coût pour vous ! Utiliser ces liens aide à soutenir ce dépôt. Nous apprécierions beaucoup si vous les utilisiez ! ❤️

Role Title: Workflow Annotator — Finance & Data Analysis  Role Type: Contractor  Location: Remote  micro1 is engaging Workflow Annotator — Finance & Data Analysis experts to contribute to a customer project focused on enhancing real-world finance and data workflows. In this role, you'll apply your expertise to help train next-generation AI systems. Your work will shape how models learn, reason, and perform through high-quality, real-world input. No prior experience in AI is required — your domain knowledge is what matters. This engagement centers on capturing, annotating, and documenting authentic, multi-step finance workflows using data warehouse and spreadsheet tools, with a strong emphasis on accuracy, realism, and security. Assignments are carried out in a contract/freelance capacity via Agora, our internal annotation platform, with flexible, self-directed participation and clear project deliverables.  Scope of Work  Design and document detailed, multi-step finance/data analyst workflows reflecting authentic tasks such as data pulls, reconciliation, model building, reporting, and analysis. Independently execute each workflow using real tools (e.g., Snowflake, Excel/Sheets), capturing every step, decision, and relevant input/output. Integrate adjacent collaboration tools naturally into workflows, such as Slack threads for cross-functional coordination or shared documents for distribution and feedback. Provide comprehensive documentation for each scenario in Agora, including clear step-by-step instructions, rationale, formulas, queries, and illustrative screenshots or results. Identify and flag ambiguous or edge-case scenarios to the project team and incorporate feedback to improve workflow accuracy and coverage. Uphold high standards of data security and privacy in all documentation and workflow execution. Strong written and verbal communication skills, with the ability to explain complex steps and context to annotators unfamiliar with the work. Familiarity with collaborative tools such as Slack and Microsoft 365/Google Workspace as part of finance/data workflows. Experience with financial modeling, forecasting, or BI dashboards (Looker, Tableau, Power BI) is a plus. Background in data annotation, process documentation, or QA is an advantage. Interested? Role Title: Data Labeling Associate - Video Content  Role Type: Contractor  Location: Remote, worldwide  micro1 is engaging Data Labeling Associate - Video Content experts to contribute to a high-impact project for a valued customer. In this role, you'll apply your expertise to help train next-generation AI systems. Your work will shape how models learn, reason, and perform through high-quality, real-world input. No prior experience in AI is required - your domain knowledge is what matters.  Scope of Work  Review and annotate short first-person POV videos depicting household tasks, focusing on precise and consistent labeling. Identify and mark hamper position as left or right of the person for each video segment. Determine and tag the exact start and end frames for each pick-and-place movement within the video. Assign and record which of the seven numbered piles each clothing item is placed into. Adhere strictly to detailed annotation guidelines provided during onboarding and throughout the engagement. Maintain high quality and consistency across multiple videos within each session, ensuring accurate, actionable data for AI training. Flag ambiguous, unclear, or unusual cases for further review in accordance with project standards.  Preferred Qualifications  Exceptional visual discrimination skills and a keen eye for subtle details and frame-level changes. Proven ability to follow complex instructions with precision and incorporate feedback promptly. Experience with web-based video tools, such as playback controls, frame stepping, and timestamp-based interfaces. Strong focus and consistency in performing repetitive, detail-oriented tasks across multiple assignments. Reliable high-speed internet connection and a computer suitable for smooth video playback.  Nice to Have (but not required)  Prior experience in video annotation, data labeling, transcription, content moderation, or QA work Familiarity with any annotation platform (CVAT, Labelbox, Scale, V7, Encord, or similar) Background in roles requiring sustained attention to repetitive detail.  Start Timeline & Availability  We typically fill roles within 48 hours, so we're looking for teammates ready to jump in. If selected, we'd love for you to start your first task as soon as you move forward with your application. The expectation is to begin within ~24 hours of completing onboarding.  Interested? Role Title: Data Labeling Associate - Video Content  Role Type: Contractor  Location: Remote, worldwide  micro1 is engaging Data Labeling Associate - Video Content experts to contribute to a high-impact project for a valued customer. In this role, you'll apply your expertise to help train next-generation AI systems. Your work will shape how models learn, reason, and perform through high-quality, real-world input. No prior experience in AI is required - your domain knowledge is what matters.  Scope of Work  Review and annotate short first-person POV videos depicting household tasks, focusing on precise and consistent labeling. Identify and mark hamper position as left or right of the person for each video segment. Determine and tag the exact start and end frames for each pick-and-place movement within the video. Assign and record which of the seven numbered piles each clothing item is placed into. Adhere strictly to detailed annotation guidelines provided during onboarding and throughout the engagement. Maintain high quality and consistency across multiple videos within each session, ensuring accurate, actionable data for AI training. Flag ambiguous, unclear, or unusual cases for further review in accordance with project standards.  Preferred Qualifications  Exceptional visual discrimination skills and a keen eye for subtle details and frame-level changes. Proven ability to follow complex instructions with precision and incorporate feedback promptly. Experience with web-based video tools, such as playback controls, frame stepping, and timestamp-based interfaces. Strong focus and consistency in performing repetitive, detail-oriented tasks across multiple assignments. Reliable high-speed internet connection and a computer suitable for smooth video playback.  Nice to Have (but not required)  Prior experience in video annotation, data labeling, transcription, content moderation, or QA work Familiarity with any annotation platform (CVAT, Labelbox, Scale, V7, Encord, or similar) Background in roles requiring sustained attention to repetitive detail.  Start Timeline & Availability  We typically fill roles within 48 hours, so we're looking for teammates ready to jump in. If selected, we'd love for you to start your first task as soon as you move forward with your application. The expectation is to begin within ~24 hours of completing onboarding.  Interested? We aim to enable 1 billion people to do meaningful work by applying their expertise to AI. As our global expert network grows, micro1 is building the human intelligence layer for frontier AI.  Role Title: Software Engineer (Go, Python, TS)  Role Type: Contractor  Location: Remote  micro1 is engaging Software Engineer (Go, Python, TS) experts to contribute to a pioneering customer project. In this role, you'll apply your expertise to help train next-generation AI systems. Your work will shape how models learn, reason, and perform through high-quality, real-world input. No prior experience in AI is required — your domain knowledge is what matters. This opportunity is ideal for contributors with a strong background in Go as their primary language, and solid proficiency in Python and TypeScript as secondary skills.  Scope of Work  Design and implement code solutions using Go, Python, and TypeScript to simulate real-world development scenarios for AI model training. Review, analyze, and critique sample code and technical materials, providing detailed written feedback that enhances AI understanding of software engineering concepts. Generate high-quality code samples, unit tests, and documentation to support a wide range of AI training use cases. Identify edge cases, best practices, and common pitfalls in software design and development to help refine model outputs. Contribute to discussions and collaborative reviews that focus on effective communication of complex technical ideas. Refine prompts and queries that will be used to evaluate and improve AI-generated code outputs.  Preferred Qualifications  Expert-level proficiency in Go, with substantial hands-on project experience. Strong command of Python and TypeScript, with the ability to write clean, idiomatic code in both languages. Excellent written and verbal communication skills, with a keen focus on articulating technical concepts clearly. Familiarity with software architecture, design patterns, and modern development workflows. Prior experience reviewing or analyzing code for correctness, efficiency, and clarity. Exposure to code documentation best practices and collaborative software projects. Interest in contributing to AI and machine learning advancements; direct AI experience is a plus but not required. Role Title: Workflow Annotator — Software Engineering  Role Type: Contractor  Location: Remote  micro1 is engaging Workflow Annotator — Software Engineering experts to contribute to a dynamic customer project focused on building a library of realistic, multi-step engineering workflows. In this role, you'll apply your expertise to help train next-generation AI systems. Your work will shape how models learn, reason, and perform through high-quality, real-world input. No prior experience in AI is required — your domain knowledge is what matters.  Scope of Work  Design and author detailed, multi-step task scenarios reflecting authentic software engineering activities, such as pull request management, issue triage, merge conflict resolution, and CI configuration updates using GitHub. Execute and document each workflow in real time within actual tools, capturing every step, rationale, edge case, and decision point to ensure comprehensive and accurate records. Integrate supplemental collaboration tools where appropriate (Slack, Microsoft 365/Google Workspace, Snowflake) to create end-to-end scenarios mirroring real engineering processes. Produce clear, structured documentation for each workflow, including step sequencing, intent at each stage, expected outcomes, and visual artifacts (screenshots, logs) as needed for effective annotation. Flag ambiguous or unrealistic scenarios to the data program team and refine workflows based on feedback to maximize realism and utility for annotation purposes. Work within the Agora internal annotation platform to submit deliverables and collaborate with project leads.  Preferred Qualifications  Minimum 2 years of professional experience in software engineering with daily use of GitHub-centered workflows. Strong command of written English, with an emphasis on clarity, consistency, and attention to detail in documenting processes for others to follow. Hands-on familiarity with Slack and Microsoft 365 or Google Workspace as part of regular engineering activities. Experience designing or participating in code review, versioning, branching, or release workflows. Exposure to CI/CD pipelines, code review platforms, or systems for process documentation is an advantage. Basic skills in SQL (preferably with Snowflake) are a plus, though not required. Prior experience with data annotation, technical writing, or QA documentation is a bonus. Interested? Role Title: Mechanical Design / CAD Expert  Role Type: Contractor  Location: Remote  micro1 is engaging Mechanical Design / CAD Experts to contribute their expertise to a challenging customer project focused on advanced engineering solutions. In this role, you'll apply your expertise to help train next-generation AI systems. Your work will shape how models learn, reason, and perform through high-quality, real-world input. No prior experience in AI is required — your domain knowledge is what matters.  Scope of Work  Provide detailed input on mechanical design solutions, including component and assembly modeling using advanced CAD tools. Evaluate and critique engineering drawings for accuracy, manufacturability, and adherence to GD&T standards. Assess and provide recommendations for design for manufacturability (DFM) and tolerance analysis practices. Contribute insights on engineering mechanics, tribology, and geometric modeling challenges relevant to real-world applications. Review and enhance graphics and geometric modeling representations for mechanical assemblies and systems. Offer guidance on best practices in manufacturing engineering and help simulate realistic design scenarios. Create written and verbal explanations of mechanical engineering concepts to inform the development and training of AI models.  Preferred Qualifications  BS or MS in Mechanical or Aerospace Engineering with a strong academic foundation. Substantial experience in production design, including hands-on use of GD&T, tolerance analysis, and DFM principles. Proficiency with industry-standard CAD platforms (e.g., SolidWorks, CATIA, NX) and demonstrated 3D modeling expertise. Strong understanding of tribology, engineering mechanics, and geometric dimensioning and tolerancing. Experience in manufacturing engineering and knowledge of end-to-end product development. Excellent written and verbal communication skills, with an ability to clearly convey technical concepts and feedback. CSWP or CSWE certification is a plus, reflecting advanced proficiency in CAD and design workflows.  Compensation Structure  Compensation is output-based; experts are paid per task that meets the project specifications. The time required to complete work may vary depending on the expert’s experience and workflow. Minimum submission requirements apply. Experts must submit a minimum of tasks per week. Role Title: Electrical & Circuit Design Expert  Role Type: Contractor  Location: Remote  micro1 is selection Electrical & Circuit Design Experts to contribute to a customer's project focused on advancing the capabilities of AI technologies in electrical engineering. In this role, you'll apply your expertise to help train next-generation AI systems.  Your work will shape how models learn, reason, and perform through high-quality, real-world input. No prior experience in AI is required , your domain knowledge is what matters.  This is an opportunity to impact the future of engineering by ensuring that AI systems can accurately interpret, solve, and explain complex circuit design challenges across analog, digital, mixed-signal, power, and RF domains. Experts with a passion for precision, technical detail, and clear written and verbal communication will find this engagement both rewarding and influential.  Scope of Work  Evaluate and solve advanced circuit design and electrical engineering problems across multiple domains, including analog, digital, mixed-signal, power, and RF circuits. Review and assess AI-generated technical responses for technical correctness, accuracy, and engineering quality. Apply practical engineering judgment to real-world design scenarios and theoretical questions. Analyze and troubleshoot a wide range of circuit types using industry-standard techniques such as Kirchhoff's laws, node-voltage analysis, small-signal analysis, and phasor analysis. Contribute domain expertise to inform model improvements and refine AI reasoning in electrical and circuit design. Document feedback and provide clear written and verbal explanations to enhance AI learning and performance. Collaborate remotely with a distributed team of experts and project participants.  Preferred Qualifications  MS or PhD in Electrical Engineering, or BS in Electrical Engineering with 8+ years of relevant industry experience. At least 5 years of hands-on experience in analog, digital, mixed-signal, power, or RF circuit design, including participation in hardware that has reached production or tape-out. Expert knowledge of analog/mixed-signal design, digital logic, semiconductor devices, power electronics, RF/microwave systems, and control systems. Proven ability to analyze complex circuits using Thevenin equivalents, Hybrid-π models, FSMs, and timing analysis. Familiarity with semiconductor reliability concerns, EMC, and modern CMOS scaling. Professional English proficiency with exceptional written and verbal communication skills. Experience with IC/ASIC design, EDA tools (Cadence, Synopsys, Mentor Graphics), PCB design, or high-speed digital/RF system validation is a plus. No prior experience in AI is required — your domain knowledge is what matters.  This opportunity centers on benchmarking advanced concepts in quantum optics, focusing on cascaded optical parametric amplifiers, SU(1,1) interferometers, and the effects of loss and two-mode squeezing. Contributors may participate as Solvers, Auditors, or Adjudicators, with assignments tailored to subfield specialization, experience, and demonstrated hands-on ability using the specified methods.  Scope of Work  Analyze and model cascaded optical parametric amplifiers and SU(1,1) interferometric systems under realistic loss mechanisms. Implement and critique loss modeling via fictitious beamsplitters, ensuring robust benchmarking against theoretical and experimental scenarios. Utilize squeeze-parameter hyperbolic identities to characterize entanglement and squeezing performance in lossy quantum systems.  Preferred Qualifications  Advanced degree (PhD or equivalent experience) in Atomic, Molecular, and Optical (AMO) Physics, Quantum Optics, or Quantum Information Science. Proven expertise with Bogoliubov transformations, covariance-matrix formalism, and quantum noise analysis. Hands-on familiarity with sideband spectra, homodyne detection, and modeling of quantum optical loss mechanisms. Demonstrated ability applying squeeze-parameter hyperbolic identities in practical research or experimental analysis. Familiarity with frontier research involving cascaded parametric amplifiers, SU(1,1) interferometry, or two-mode squeezing is highly valued. Interested? Familiarity with quality control protocols, safety standards, and regulatory guidelines is a plus. Interested? Role Title: PhD Physicist  Role Type: Contractor  Location: Remote  micro1 is engaging PhD Physicists to contribute their expertise to a customer’s project focused on advanced physics research and AI training. High level of self-motivation and commitment to delivering quality work independently.  Preferred Qualifications:  Degree in Linguistics, Philology, Translation Studies, or related field. Experience with linguistic annotation or corpus analysis tools. Background in educational, research, or media translation projects. Interested? Experts contribute their diverse subject matter knowledge across domains such as finance, healthcare, STEM engineering, and more. micro1 transforms that real-world expertise into high-quality training data, evaluations, and feedback loops that improve how AI systems learn, reason, and perform.  Our platform identifies and vets top talent through an AI recruiter, enabling high-quality expert contributions at scale. We aim to enable 1 billion people to do meaningful work by applying their expertise to AI. As our global expert network grows, micro1 is building the human intelligence layer for frontier AI.  Role Title: Biologist  Role Type: Contractor  Location: Remote  micro1 is engaging Biologists to contribute their cellular and molecular biology expertise to an innovative customer project. In this role, you'll apply your expertise to help train next-generation AI systems. Your work will shape how models learn, reason, and perform through high-quality, real-world input. No prior experience in AI is required — your domain knowledge is what matters. This opportunity places a strong emphasis on both written and verbal communication, as you will help define and assess expert-level scientific content. Professionals with hands-on wet lab and CRISPR experience are especially encouraged to participate.  Key Responsibilities:  Lead the design, optimization, and execution of CRISPR-based gene editing experiments. Develop and implement cloning strategies to generate novel constructs and cell lines. Perform genotyping, PCR-based assays, and sequence analyses to validate genetic modifications. Design synthetic DNA/RNA sequences and contribute to screening logic for high-throughput workflows. Collaborate with interdisciplinary teams to translate experimental data into structured formats for AI model training. Apply molecular biology techniques to study and characterize molecules, organelles, and cellular organization. Document experimental procedures and results with exceptional clarity and precision, supporting reproducibility and knowledge transfer.   > Required skills: live music performance, musical instrument proficiency, public performance, 7+, $100-300/h.  live music performance musical instrument proficiency public performance event logistics coordination self-promotion (e.g., sharing portfolio or social media links) time management permit and compliance awareness audio/video recording compliance professionalism adaptability About micro1 micro1 is the leading AI data lab for training frontier models and evaluating AI agents. We aim to enable 1 billion people to do meaningful work by applying their expertise to AI. As our global expert network grows, micro1 is building the human intelligence layer for frontier AI.  About Us  At micro1, we work with human experts across a wide range of fields to build more reliable AI systems. Human expertise is at the center of everything we do, and we believe the best technology creates more opportunities for people to create, perform, and discover.  In the first edition of microsessions, we're inviting local musicians to perform live in outdoor areas across San Francisco on August 6–7. We'll sponsor artists to play in some of the city's most iconic public spaces, introduce their music to new audiences, and professionally capture their performances as part of a project exploring the future of music education.  We are creating this as something musicians enjoy being part of, and something San Francisco enjoys stumbling upon.  What You'll Be Doing  We're looking for 20–40 solo musicians and small groups (1–3 performers) to perform live across San Francisco on Thursday, August 6 and Friday, August 7.  Selected musicians will:  Perform a 1–2 hour live set during one of three daily time slots: LUNCH - 11:00 AM – 1:00 PM AFTERNOON - 2:00 PM – 4:00 PM HAPPY HOUR - 5:00 PM – 8:00 PM Perform at one of several high-foot-traffic locations across the city, including FiDi, the Marina, Dolores Park, Alamo Square, Pier 39, and Golden Gate Park (locations will be finalized with each musician before the event but musicians must ultimately secure their desired locations and any associated logistics) Wear micro1 event apparel during the performance + feature basic signage explaining microsessions. Be filmed and recorded by a small production crew for approximately 20-60 minutes during their set. About the Recording - What It's For (and What It's Not)  Every performance will be filmed and recorded. We're upfront about this on-site and in this brief because we think a project about honoring musicians should be honest with everyone it touches. Here's exactly what the recording is used for:  Marketing: a launch film and social content celebrating the artists and the city. Education-focused research: audio can help AI models better understand music — things like recognizing instruments and genres, learning music theory (keys, chord progressions), and understanding the cultural roots of a style, as well as potentially enhancing artist discoverability. The goal is tools that help people learn and discover music, not replace musicians or their work.  What we will never do:  Use this data to generate artificial music, ever. We don’t allow it and we don’t appreciate it. We are making a public promise to reserve this data for tools that support musicians, never extract from them. Use it to replace or compete with musicians. Use it for anything beyond the education and marketing purposes described above.  Musicians are compensated and every performer participates with full consent and a signed release covering the licensing described here.  Who We're Looking For Solo musicians or groups of 1–3 people. Available for a 1–2 hour set within one of our time slots between August 6–7 (playing on both days is welcome if you're interested). Able to perform at a location you're already permitted or approved to play at (whether by city permit or private-property arrangement) — final locations are subject to our approval but we are not able to coordinate permits at this time. Responsible for your own instruments/equipment; please build any related costs into your quoted rate. Able to share a video, Instagram, or portfolio link showing a past performance. Comfortable being filmed and recorded per the terms above. Interested? Título del Puesto: Graba Videos desde Casa (Estados Unidos)  Tipo de Contratación: Contratista (Contractor)  Ubicación: Remoto – Candidatos ubicados en los siguientes estados:  Alabama, Alaska, Arkansas, Delaware, Florida, Georgia, Idaho, Indiana, Iowa, Kansas, Kentucky, Louisiana, Maryland, Massachusetts, Michigan, Minnesota, Mississippi, Missouri, Montana, Nebraska, Nevada, New Hampshire, Nuevo México, Carolina del Norte, Dakota del Norte, Ohio, Oklahoma, Pensilvania, Carolina del Sur, Dakota del Sur, Tennessee, Utah, Vermont, Virginia, Virginia Occidental, Wisconsin y Wyoming.  Resumen del Puesto  En este rol, utilizarás tus habilidades para contribuir al entrenamiento de sistemas de Inteligencia Artificial de última generación. Tu trabajo ayudará directamente a que estos modelos aprendan, razonen y ejecuten tareas mediante datos reales y de alta calidad.  No es necesario tener experiencia previa en Inteligencia Artificial. Lo más importante es tu capacidad para seguir instrucciones y generar datos de alta calidad.  Responsabilidades Principales  Capturar datos precisos de movimiento utilizando los sensores IMU de tu smartphone mientras realizas actividades físicas específicas. Grabar videos sincronizados para garantizar la precisión de los datos y la integridad de toda la información de movimiento recopilada. Seguir protocolos técnicos detallados para cumplir de manera consistente con los estándares de calidad, etiquetado y estandarización del proyecto. Entregar un mínimo de 10 horas semanales de videos aprobados y de alta calidad durante todo el proyecto. Comunicarte de forma clara y proactiva con el equipo para resolver dudas y compartir observaciones sobre el proceso de recolección de datos. Cumplir de manera constante con los plazos y entregables establecidos por el proyecto. Participar en una verificación de compatibilidad del dispositivo y en un proceso de entrevista asistido por IA durante el onboarding.  Requisitos  Contar con una banda para sujetar el celular en la cabeza (head strap), capaz de capturar ambas manos en el video, dentro de las 48 horas posteriores a la fecha de inicio. Tener un smartphone compatible. Los dispositivos compatibles incluyen: iPhone 12 o superior. Google Pixel 6 o superior. Samsung Galaxy S21 o superior. Excelente atención al detalle y capacidad demostrada para seguir procedimientos estandarizados. Capacidad para gestionar entregas de forma consistente y cumplir plazos en un entorno dinámico y orientado a datos. Excelentes habilidades de comunicación escrita y verbal para documentar información y colaborar de forma remota. Facilidad para utilizar tecnología y experiencia con dispositivos móviles para la captura de video y datos de sensores. Condición física para realizar tareas repetitivas de movimiento de manera segura y eficiente. Acceso a un smartphone compatible capaz de recopilar datos con alta fidelidad.  Nota: Las postulaciones enviadas con una dirección de correo electrónico de Gmail serán consideradas un valor agregado, ya que facilitan la integración con las herramientas del proyecto.  Cualificaciones Deseables  Formación o experiencia en robótica, kinesiología, recolección de datos mediante sensores o áreas técnicas relacionadas. Experiencia en análisis del movimiento humano, segmentación de actividades o etiquetado de conjuntos de datos para aprendizaje automático (Machine Learning). Familiaridad con la organización y estructuración de grandes volúmenes de datos para aplicaciones de IA y robótica.  Estructura de Compensación  La compensación se basa en la producción. Los contratistas reciben un pago por cada video que cumpla con las especificaciones del proyecto.  El tiempo necesario para completar las tareas puede variar según la experiencia y el flujo de trabajo de cada participante.  Existen requisitos mínimos de entrega: los videos deben cumplir con la duración especificada por el proyecto y cada participante deberá enviar entre 10 y 15 horas de video por semana.  Inicio de Actividades y Disponibilidad  Normalmente completamos el proceso de selección en un plazo de 48 horas, por lo que buscamos personas con disponibilidad para comenzar rápidamente.  Si eres seleccionado(a), esperamos que inicies tu primera tarea tan pronto avances en el proceso de contratación.  Se espera que el inicio de las actividades ocurra aproximadamente 24 horas después de completar el onboarding.  Requisitos de Equipamiento  Smartphone  Todas las tareas del proyecto deberán realizarse utilizando un smartphone.  Los participantes grabarán su trabajo directamente desde el dispositivo móvil mientras realizan las actividades asignadas.  Banda para la Cabeza (Head Strap)  Las tareas requieren el uso de una cámara colocada en la cabeza mediante una banda para celular (head strap).  Los participantes grabarán videos en primera persona mientras realizan las actividades físicas.  Las especificaciones de la banda requerida y de cualquier equipo adicional se compartirán durante el proceso de onboarding. Interested? Captura de Vídeo Atenção a Detalhes Tarefas domésticas About micro1 micro1 is the leading AI data lab for training frontier models and evaluating AI agents. Experts contribute their diverse subject matter knowledge across domains such as finance, healthcare, STEM engineering, and more. micro1 transforms that real-world expertise into high-quality training data, evaluations, and feedback loops that improve how AI systems learn, reason, and perform.  Our platform identifies and vets top talent through an AI recruiter, enabling high-quality expert contributions at scale. We aim to enable 1 billion people to do meaningful work by applying their expertise to AI. As our global expert network grows, micro1 is building the human intelligence layer for frontier AI.  Título da Vaga: Gravador(a) de Vídeos em Casa (Estados Unidos)  Tipo de Contratação: Contractor  Localização: Remoto – Profissionais baseados nos seguintes estados: Alabama, Alaska, Arkansas, Delaware, Florida, Georgia, Idaho, Indiana, Iowa, Kansas, Kentucky, Louisiana, Maryland, Massachusetts, Michigan, Minnesota, Mississippi, Missouri, Montana, Nebraska, Nevada, New Hampshire, New Mexico, North Carolina, North Dakota, Ohio, Oklahoma, Pennsylvania, South Carolina, South Dakota, Tennessee, Utah, Vermont, Virginia, West Virginia, Wisconsin, and Wyoming.  Resumo da Vaga:  Nesta função, você utilizará sua experiência para ajudar no treinamento de sistemas de IA de última geração. Seu trabalho contribuirá diretamente para a forma como esses modelos aprendem, raciocinam e executam tarefas por meio de dados reais e de alta qualidade. Não é necessário ter experiência prévia com Inteligência Artificial — o mais importante é sua capacidade de seguir instruções e produzir dados de qualidade.  Principais Responsabilidades  Capturar dados precisos de movimento utilizando os sensores IMU do seu smartphone durante atividades físicas especificadas. Gravar vídeos sincronizados para garantir a precisão dos dados e a integridade de todas as informações de movimento coletadas. Seguir protocolos técnicos detalhados para atender consistentemente aos padrões de qualidade, rotulagem e padronização do projeto. Entregar no mínimo 10 horas semanais de vídeos aprovados e de alta qualidade durante todo o período do projeto. Comunicar-se de forma clara e proativa com a equipe para esclarecer dúvidas e compartilhar observações sobre o processo de coleta de dados. Garantir entregas pontuais e consistentes, em conformidade com os marcos e expectativas do projeto. Participar da verificação de compatibilidade do dispositivo e de um processo de entrevista inovador com suporte de IA durante o onboarding.  Requisitos  Ter acesso a uma faixa para fixação do celular na cabeça (head strap), capaz de registrar ambas as mãos, em até 48 horas após a data de início. Possuir um smartphone compatível. Dispositivos suportados incluem: iPhone 12 ou superior; Google Pixel 6 ou superior; Samsung Galaxy S21 ou superior. Excelente atenção aos detalhes e capacidade comprovada de seguir protocolos padronizados. Capacidade de gerenciar entregas de forma consistente e cumprir prazos em um ambiente dinâmico e orientado por dados. Excelentes habilidades de comunicação escrita e verbal para documentação e colaboração remota. Facilidade com tecnologia e experiência no uso de dispositivos móveis para captura de vídeo e dados de sensores. Condições físicas para realizar tarefas repetitivas de movimento com segurança e eficiência. Acesso a um smartphone compatível capaz de realizar coleta de dados com alta fidelidade.  Observação: Candidaturas enviadas utilizando um endereço de e-mail Gmail serão consideradas um diferencial, pois facilitam a integração com as ferramentas do projeto.  Qualificações Desejáveis  Formação ou experiência em robótica, cinesiologia, coleta de dados por sensores ou áreas técnicas relacionadas. Experiência com análise de movimento humano, segmentação de atividades ou rotulagem de conjuntos de dados para aprendizado de máquina. Familiaridade com organização e estruturação de grandes conjuntos de dados para aplicações de IA e robótica.  Estrutura de Remuneração  A remuneração é baseada na produção. Os profissionais são pagos por cada vídeo gravado que atenda às especificações do projeto. O tempo necessário para concluir as atividades pode variar de acordo com a experiência e o fluxo de trabalho de cada participante.  Há requisitos mínimos de entrega: os vídeos devem possuir a duração especificada pelo projeto, e cada profissional deverá enviar entre 10 e 15 horas de vídeo por semana.  Início das Atividades e Disponibilidade  Normalmente concluímos o processo de seleção em até 48 horas, portanto buscamos profissionais disponíveis para iniciar rapidamente. Caso seja selecionado(a), esperamos que você comece sua primeira tarefa assim que avançar no processo de candidatura. A expectativa é que o início das atividades ocorra aproximadamente 24 horas após a conclusão do onboarding .  Requisitos de Equipamentos  Smartphone  Todas as tarefas deste projeto deverão ser realizadas utilizando um smartphone. Os participantes gravarão seu fluxo de trabalho diretamente pelo dispositivo móvel enquanto executam as atividades.  Faixa para Cabeça (Head Strap)  As tarefas exigem o uso de uma câmera posicionada na cabeça por meio de uma faixa (head strap). Os participantes gravarão vídeos em primeira pessoa enquanto realizam as atividades físicas. As especificações da faixa necessária e de quaisquer equipamentos adicionais serão compartilhadas durante o processo de onboarding. Interested? We seek professionals with a strong background in recording professional-grade audio, preferably with experience delivering content for linguistics, voice technology, or media production purposes. Experience working on remote projects or in distributed teams, maintaining accountability and timely delivery of audio assets. Familiarity with best practices in audio file management, labeling, and secure digital transfer.  Please note that you will need your professional audio equipment during the interview.  Interested? Role Title: BIM & Construction Coordination Expert  Role Type: Contractor  Location: Remote  micro1 is engaging BIM & Construction Coordination Experts to contribute their expertise on a dynamic customer project. In this role, you'll apply your expertise to help train next-generation AI systems. Your work will shape how models learn, reason, and perform through high-quality, real-world input. No prior experience in AI is required — your domain knowledge is what matters. This opportunity involves reviewing and analyzing architectural and engineering blueprints, identifying design and construction clashes, and providing insightful explanations and documentation to support advanced AI training for construction workflows.  Scope of Work  Analyze and review architectural and engineering blueprints to identify design and construction clashes. Utilize Autodesk Revit and Navisworks to perform BIM coordination and execute clash detection processes. Document and explain the root cause of identified issues, providing detailed reasoning and solution recommendations. Prepare and deliver clear, comprehensive technical documentation supporting construction coordination processes. Collaborate via written and verbal channels to communicate complex coordination challenges and solutions. Contribute expert insight into best practices for BIM execution and construction drawing interpretation. Participate in quality assurance of construction documentation to ensure consistency and accuracy for AI model evaluation.  Preferred Qualifications  Extensive experience with BIM coordination in real-world construction projects. Proficiency in Autodesk Revit and Navisworks, especially for clash detection and construction drawing analysis. Strong analytical reasoning and problem-solving skills applied to construction coordination. Demonstrated expertise in reviewing and interpreting blueprints, technical documents, and engineering drawings. Exceptional written and verbal communication abilities, with a care for clarity and thoroughness. Background in documenting construction workflows, issue logs, or technical resolutions. Familiarity with AI applications in the construction sector is a plus, but not required. Interested?
